# Fetch failure log

Write-only debug log appended by `fetch_papers.py` (`log_failed()`). Nothing in the codebase
reads this file back — it exists purely for a human to scan if a fetch run behaves oddly.

Older entries were archived to keep this file readable: see `SOURCES_FAILED.archive-2026-09-06.md`
for everything before 2026-09-06. That archive is safe to delete if you never need to look at it —
it is not read by any script.
